# History Form 3, Form Three History: authored answers for review

Every answer fixed by a person, not computed. Part 1: the tables, conventions and rules that computed answers come from.
Part 2: every authored question template, with all its data (keys, statements, pairs, choices and feedback) and sources.
Regenerate with `npm run review:f3-history`. Item IDs (TR-…) match `docs/teacher-review.md`.

## Part 1

Rules the computed answers use are listed in the subject’s library tests; every authored answer is in Part 2.

### `y3-1-terms` · Lesson 1 · multiple choice, level 1

Prompt: {X.q}

Table `X` (one row is picked each time):

| q | r | w | e | y |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Which term means: political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions ? | centralised authority | distributed authority · production · intermediary trade | distributed authority means political roles shared among several local institutions; the question instead describes political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions. · production means activities providing material goods and livelihoods; the question instead describes political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions. · intermediary trade means exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks; the question instead describes political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions. | Political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions. |
| Which term means: political roles shared among several local institutions ? | distributed authority | centralised authority · production · intermediary trade | centralised authority means political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions; the question instead describes political roles shared among several local institutions. · production means activities providing material goods and livelihoods; the question instead describes political roles shared among several local institutions. · intermediary trade means exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks; the question instead describes political roles shared among several local institutions. | Political roles shared among several local institutions. |
| Which term means: activities providing material goods and livelihoods ? | production | centralised authority · distributed authority · intermediary trade | centralised authority means political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions; the question instead describes activities providing material goods and livelihoods. · distributed authority means political roles shared among several local institutions; the question instead describes activities providing material goods and livelihoods. · intermediary trade means exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks; the question instead describes activities providing material goods and livelihoods. | Activities providing material goods and livelihoods. |
| Which term means: exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks ? | intermediary trade | centralised authority · distributed authority · production | centralised authority means political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions; the question instead describes exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks. · distributed authority means political roles shared among several local institutions; the question instead describes exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks. · production means activities providing material goods and livelihoods; the question instead describes exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks. | Exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks. |

Right answer: `{X.r}`; wrong choices: `{X.w[0]}`, `{X.w[1]}`, `{X.w[2]}`

- Feedback `w0`: “{X.e[0]}”
- Feedback `w1`: “{X.e[1]}”
- Feedback `w2`: “{X.e[2]}”

Three generated variants:

> Which term means: political roles shared among several local institutions ?

- ◻️ intermediary trade  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: intermediary trade means exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks; the question instead describes political roles shared among several local institutions._
- ◻️ centralised authority  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: centralised authority means political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions; the question instead describes political roles shared among several local institutions._

  _↳ feedback if chosen: production means activities providing material goods and livelihoods; the question instead describes political roles shared among several local institutions._

- ◻️ intermediary trade

Full working after a second miss:

> Political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions.  
> So the answer is “centralised authority”.  

> Which term means: political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions ?

- ◻️ intermediary trade

- ✅ centralised authority
- ◻️ distributed authority

### `y3-1-repair` · Lesson 1 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: Find the incorrect claim.

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ centralised authority: political power concentrated in a ruler and supporting institutions.
- ✅ distributed authority: political roles shared among several local institutions.
- ✅ production: activities providing material goods and livelihoods.
- ✅ intermediary trade: exchange connecting different producer and buyer networks.
- ❌ All precolonial communities lacked political institutions.  
  _↳ Courts, rulers and other institutions existed in several different forms._
- ❌ A community without one central monarch must have no government.  
  _↳ Authority can be organised through several institutions rather than one monarch._
- ❌ Economic activity began only with colonial rule.  
  _↳ Farming and crafts were established activities before colonial annexation._
- ❌ A trading intermediary has no role between producer and overseas buyer.

### `y3-4-repair` · Lesson 4 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: Find the incorrect claim.

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ exploration record: travel knowledge and mapping collected during an expedition.
- ✅ missionary activity: religious work often linked to education or translation.
- ✅ commercial enterprise: business activity seeking returns from exchange or production.
- ✅ imperial enforcement: official or military action extending colonial authority.
- ❌ A European map proves that local people knew nothing about their land.  
  _↳ Recorded maps often depended on existing African knowledge._
- ❌ Schooling alone proves every mission opposed all imperial interests.  
  _↳ Activities and political relationships varied; one activity does not settle every motive._
- ❌ A company station is necessarily a charitable project with no commercial aims.  
  _↳ Commercial enterprises seek economic returns, even when they also claim other aims._

Right-hand side (shuffled): A local ruler cooperates with colonial agents against a rival. · Soldiers enter a resisting locality to enforce a colonial demand. · A claimed area contains communities that oppose imposed demands.
- military expedition → **Soldiers enter a resisting locality to enforce a colonial demand.**
- alliance → **A local ruler cooperates with colonial agents against a rival.**
- contested expansion → **A claimed area contains communities that oppose imposed demands.**

Full working after a second miss:

> The right pairs are:  
> military expedition → Soldiers enter a resisting locality to enforce a colonial demand.  
> alliance → A local ruler cooperates with colonial agents against a rival.  
> contested expansion → A claimed area contains communities that oppose imposed demands.

Right-hand side (shuffled): A resident directs policy and can challenge a ruler's conduct. · A government with few European officials works through established offices. · A recognised emir and officials carry out administrative functions.
- administrative economy → **A government with few European officials works through established offices.**
- colonial supervision → **A resident directs policy and can challenge a ruler's conduct.**
- local intermediary authority → **A recognised emir and officials carry out administrative functions.**
